-> Strategy brain, version 2:
Fixed-limit hold'em, 4 players. Tight-aggressive, avoid multiway junk.
- Preflop: raise premium (AA-QQ, AK, JJ-TT). Broadway and suited connectors (TJs, JQ) raise in late position or as BB with limpers. Small pairs and suited aces: call cheap, late position only. Fold weak offsuit (T7, 52s, 4T) to any raise, especially out of position.
- Call only when price is under about 1/4 of pot. Do not cold-call raises with weak hands.
- Postflop: value-bet and raise sets, two pair, top pair. Pair with a draw: call. Board-only pairs: check and call small bets; fold to big multi-street pressure.
- Opponents: A01 raises preflop and bets rivers with real hands; respect its bets. A04 calls wide and rarely bets; value bet it thinly. A02 check-calls, raises turns with strong hands. Most pots check down; bluff rarely.

-> Strategy brain, version 4:
Fixed-limit hold'em, 4 players, 8-hand games. Tight, disciplined, avoid multiway junk.
- Preflop: raise premium (AA-QQ, AK, JJ-TT). Suited broadway/connectors (TJs, JQs, 98s) and suited aces: call or raise cheaply in position or as BB. Fold weak offsuit (J4, K4, Q6o, 69o, 73o, Q4o) to any raise. Cheap completes (price 1-2) fine in position; small pairs call cheaply.
- Call when price is under about 1/4 of pot. Fold one-pair or ace-high hands to a raise plus a bet.
- Postflop: value-bet sets, two pair, overpairs. Pair with a draw: call. Weak pairs in multiway pots: check. Open-ended draws: call with fair price. Don't chase gutshots.
- Opponents: A02 raises first-in, often with two pair or better; respect its bets. A01 raises preflop and bets turns with real hands. A04 check-calls flops and calls with pairs; bets rivers only sometimes.
